旁

那戴着宝珠的妖兽为啥就平安无事嘞？

还有，那个陷阱是月兔设下的吗？

娜兹玲  
不不不——应该不可能。
  
  
那只月兔说自己是不小心弄丢的宝珠。
  
  
她看着根本没有设下陷阱的机会嘛
  
<img alt="二岩猯藏（兽王园立绘）.png" src="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/50px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="50" height="65" srcset="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/75px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 1.5x, https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/100px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 2x" data-file-width="710" data-file-height="920">

她也可能是装的嘛
  
<img alt="娜兹玲（兽王园立绘）.png" src="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/50px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="50" height="62" srcset="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/75px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 1.5x, https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/100px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 2x" data-file-width="730" data-file-height="901">
娜兹玲  
这样哦？  
不过，宝珠的苦头我已经吃得够够的了——
  
<img alt="二岩猯藏（兽王园立绘）.png" src="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/50px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="50" height="65" srcset="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/75px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 1.5x, https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/c/ca/%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/100px-%E4%BA%8C%E5%B2%A9%E7%8C%AF%E8%97%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 2x" data-file-width="710" data-file-height="920">
二岩猯藏  
你这宝藏猎人怎么就打起退堂鼓啦
  
<img alt="娜兹玲（兽王园立绘）.png" src="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/50px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="50" height="62" srcset="https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/75px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 1.5x, https://upload.thwiki.cc/thumb/3/30/%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png/100px-%E5%A8%9C%E5%85%B9%E7%8E%B2%EF%BC%88%E5%85%BD%E7%8E%8B%E5%9B%AD%E7%AB%8B%E7%BB%98%EF%BC%89.png 2x" data-file-width="730" data-file-height="901">
娜兹玲  
毕竟我可不想变成  
拿着宝珠的妖兽那样啊——
  
旁  
娜兹玲之所以打起了退堂鼓，是出于对地狱的畏惧。
  
  
对封闭在地底深处的那段回忆，  
她有着本能的恐惧。
  
  
她放弃了寻找宝珠，专心投身到打扫墓地、驱逐幽灵的工作中去。
  
  
然而，幽灵的泛滥和宝珠一样，原本就处在这场异变的漩涡中心。
  
  
到头来，娜兹玲还是被卷入了异变之中。
  
旁  
Ending　No.06　　娜兹玲的心灵创伤
  
  
Congratulation!  
恭喜通关！
  
旁旁白
